- [ ] **Step 12: 建立 `backend/app/rag/store.py`**

```python
"""向量庫：Chroma 的非同步 HTTP 客戶端（chromadb-client）。每個品牌一個 collection（{brandId}_ci），距離為 cosine。

只有 worker 寫入；資料以 PostgreSQL 為準，Chroma 只是索引（見 sync.py）。Chroma 的錯誤一律轉成 RagError。
"""

import asyncio
import contextlib
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import chromadb
import httpx
from chromadb.api import AsyncClientAPI
from chromadb.api.models.AsyncCollection import AsyncCollection
from chromadb.api.types import PyEmbedding
from chromadb.config import DEFAULT_DATABASE
from chromadb.errors import NotFoundError

from app.rag.errors import RagError

#: collection 名稱：{brandId}_ci
SUFFIX = '_ci'
#: 每次呼叫 Chroma 最多等待的秒數（chromadb-client 的 HTTP 連線沒有逾時）
TIMEOUT = 30

#: Chroma 的 metadata 條件，例如 {'origin': {'$ne': 'alt'}}；兩個以上的條件要包在 $and 或 $or 裡
Where = dict[str, Any]


def collection_name(brand_id: str) -> str:
    return f'{brand_id}{SUFFIX}'


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VectorRecord:
    id: str
    embedding: list[float]
    #: 切塊的內文
    document: str
    metadata: dict[str, str]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VectorHit:
    id: str
    document: str
    metadata: dict[str, str]
    #: cosine 相似度（1 - distance）
    score: float


def _strings(metadata: Mapping[str, object] | None) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {key: str(value) for key, value in (metadata or {}).items()}


class VectorStore:
    def __init__(self, url: str, *, database: str = DEFAULT_DATABASE) -> None:
        self.url = url
        self.database = database
        self._client: AsyncClientAPI | None = None
        self._connecting = asyncio.Lock()

    async def upsert(self, brand_id: str, records: Sequence[VectorRecord]) -> None:
        """一次最多 5461 筆（Chroma 的上限）。"""
        if not records:
            return
        embeddings: list[PyEmbedding] = [r.embedding for r in records]
        await self._run(
            brand_id,
            lambda c: c.upsert(
                ids=[r.id for r in records],
                embeddings=embeddings,
                metadatas=[r.metadata for r in records],
                documents=[r.document for r in records],
            ),
        )

    async def delete(self, brand_id: str, *, ids: Sequence[str] | None = None, where: Where | None = None) -> None:
        """依 id 或 metadata 條件刪除；沒有符合的切塊時不做任何事。"""
        if ids is not None and not ids:
            return
        await self._run(brand_id, lambda c: c.delete(ids=list(ids) if ids is not None else None, where=where))

    async def metadata(self, brand_id: str) -> dict[str, dict[str, str]]:
        """每個切塊的 metadata（id → metadata）：同步時與 PostgreSQL 比對，不讀向量。"""
        result = await self._run(brand_id, lambda c: c.get(include=['metadatas']))
        return {id_: _strings(m) for id_, m in zip(result['ids'], result['metadatas'] or [], strict=True)}

    async def query(
        self, brand_id: str, embedding: Sequence[float], k: int, where: Where | None = None
    ) -> list[VectorHit]:
        """與 embedding 最相近的 k 個切塊，依相似度由高到低。"""
        query: list[PyEmbedding] = [list(embedding)]
        result = await self._run(
            brand_id,
            lambda c: c.query(
                query_embeddings=query,
                n_results=k,
                where=where,
                include=['metadatas', 'documents', 'distances'],
            ),
        )
        ids, documents = result['ids'][0], (result['documents'] or [[]])[0]
        metadatas, distances = (result['metadatas'] or [[]])[0], (result['distances'] or [[]])[0]
        return [
            VectorHit(id_, doc, _strings(m), 1 - d)
            for id_, doc, m, d in zip(ids, documents, metadatas, distances, strict=True)
        ]

    async def drop(self, brand_id: str) -> None:
        """刪除品牌的 collection；本來就沒有時不做任何事。"""

        async def drop(client: AsyncClientAPI) -> None:
            with contextlib.suppress(NotFoundError):
                await client.delete_collection(collection_name(brand_id))

        await self._call(drop)

    async def brands(self) -> list[str]:
        """有 collection 的品牌 id。"""

        async def names(client: AsyncClientAPI) -> list[str]:
            return [c.name for c in await client.list_collections()]

        return sorted(n.removesuffix(SUFFIX) for n in await self._call(names) if n.endswith(SUFFIX))

    async def _run[T](self, brand_id: str, call: Callable[[AsyncCollection], Awaitable[T]]) -> T:
        """在品牌的 collection 上執行（沒有時建立）。不保留 collection 物件：collection 被刪除後舊的物件就不能用。"""

        async def run(client: AsyncClientAPI) -> T:
            collection = await client.get_or_create_collection(
                collection_name(brand_id), configuration={'hnsw': {'space': 'cosine'}}, embedding_function=None
            )
            return await call(collection)

        return await self._call(run)

    async def _call[T](self, call: Callable[[AsyncClientAPI], Awaitable[T]]) -> T:
        try:
            async with asyncio.timeout(TIMEOUT):
                return await call(await self._connect())
        except RagError:
            raise
        except TimeoutError as error:
            raise RagError(f'向量庫（Chroma）{TIMEOUT} 秒內沒有回應（{self.url}）') from error
        except httpx.TransportError as error:
            raise self._unreachable(error) from error
        except Exception as error:
            raise RagError(f'向量庫（Chroma）操作失敗：{error}') from error

    async def _connect(self) -> AsyncClientAPI:
        async with self._connecting:
            if self._client is None:
                url = httpx.URL(self.url)
                ssl = url.scheme == 'https'
                try:
                    self._client = await chromadb.AsyncHttpClient(
                        host=url.host, port=url.port or (443 if ssl else 80), ssl=ssl, database=self.database
                    )
                except (httpx.TransportError, ValueError) as error:
                    # 建立客戶端時會先連線確認 tenant 與 database；連不上時 chromadb 可能改丟 ValueError
                    raise self._unreachable(error) from error
            return self._client

    def _unreachable(self, error: Exception) -> RagError:
        return RagError(f'向量庫（Chroma）無法連線（{self.url}）：{error}')
```

- [ ] **Step 13: 建立 `backend/app/rag/sync.py`**

```python
"""PostgreSQL → Chroma 的同步：brand_chunks 為準，Chroma 只是索引，資料遺失時會自動重建。

比對 Chroma 中每個切塊的 metadata：缺少或不同的重新向量化後 upsert，PostgreSQL 已沒有的刪除。
沒有差異時只查一次資料庫與一次 Chroma，不會載入模型。
"""

import asyncio
import hashlib
import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass
from itertools import batched

from app.contract import RagChunk
from app.db.session import Database
from app.rag.engine import RagEngine
from app.rag.store import VectorRecord, VectorStore
from app.repo import catalog

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

#: 每向量化幾個切塊就寫入 Chroma 一次：重建大量切塊時中途失敗，已完成的不必重算
UPSERT_BATCH = 32


def index_text(chunk: RagChunk) -> str:
    """向量化的文字：章節名稱也帶有語意，例如「§2.1 色彩系統」。"""
    return f'{chunk.section}：{chunk.text}'


def text_hash(text: str) -> str:
    """向量化文字的 SHA-256 前 16 碼。"""
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]


def chunk_metadata(brand_id: str, chunk: RagChunk, origin: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    """寫進 Chroma 的 metadata。同步時比對整份 metadata：內文、章節、tag、source 任何一項改變都會重新寫入。"""
    return {
        'brandId': brand_id,
        'tag': chunk.tag,
        'source': chunk.source,
        'section': chunk.section,
        'origin': origin,
        'textHash': text_hash(index_text(chunk)),
    }


async def vector_record(engine: RagEngine, brand_id: str, chunk: RagChunk, origin: str) -> VectorRecord:
    """向量化一個切塊（上傳手冊、歷史回寫也用這個寫入 Chroma）。"""
    embedding = await engine.embed(index_text(chunk))
    return VectorRecord(chunk.id, embedding, chunk.text, chunk_metadata(brand_id, chunk, origin))


class BrandLocks:
    """worker 行程內每個品牌一把鎖。同步、上傳與回寫寫入 Chroma 前都要取得，避免同步時誤刪剛寫入的切塊。"""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._locks: dict[str, asyncio.Lock] = {}

    def __call__(self, brand_id: str) -> asyncio.Lock:
        return self._locks.setdefault(brand_id, asyncio.Lock())


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SyncResult:
    #: 重新向量化並寫入的切塊數（新增或內容改變）
    embedded: int
    #: 從 Chroma 刪除的切塊數
    removed: int


async def sync_brand(
    db: Database, store: VectorStore, engine: RagEngine, locks: BrandLocks, brand_id: str
) -> SyncResult:
    """比對並修正一個品牌（持有品牌鎖）。"""
    async with locks(brand_id):
        async with db.session() as s:
            chunks = await catalog.list_chunks(s, brand_id)
        stored = await store.metadata(brand_id)
        wanted = {c.id for c, _ in chunks}
        removed = [id_ for id_ in stored if id_ not in wanted]
        await store.delete(brand_id, ids=removed)
        changed = [(c, origin) for c, origin in chunks if stored.get(c.id) != chunk_metadata(brand_id, c, origin)]
        for batch in batched(changed, UPSERT_BATCH, strict=False):
            await store.upsert(brand_id, [await vector_record(engine, brand_id, c, origin) for c, origin in batch])
        return SyncResult(embedded=len(changed), removed=len(removed))


async def sync_all(db: Database, store: VectorStore, engine: RagEngine, locks: BrandLocks) -> None:
    """worker 啟動時：依序同步所有啟用中的品牌，並刪除已停用品牌的 collection。"""
    async with db.session() as s:
        active = await catalog.active_brand_ids(s)
    changed = False
    for brand_id in active:
        result = await sync_brand(db, store, engine, locks, brand_id)
        if result.embedded or result.removed:
            changed = True
            logger.info('RAG 同步 %s：向量化 %d 個切塊、刪除 %d 個', brand_id, result.embedded, result.removed)
    for brand_id in await store.brands():
        if brand_id not in active:
            changed = True
            async with locks(brand_id):
                await store.drop(brand_id)
            logger.info('RAG 同步：刪除已停用品牌 %s 的 collection', brand_id)
    if not changed:
        # 沒有差異時也留一行紀錄，重新啟動 worker 後可以確認沒有重新向量化
        logger.info('RAG 同步完成：%d 個品牌的索引都沒有差異', len(active))
```

### Task 5：任務流程改用 BGE-M3＋Chroma 檢索

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/rag/retriever.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/pipeline/ports.py`、`backend/app/pipeline/runner.py`、`backend/app/pipeline/sim.py`、`backend/app/rag/sync.py`、`backend/app/worker.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/conftest.py`、`backend/tests/test_rag_retriever.py`、`backend/tests/test_pipeline.py`、`backend/tests/test_worker.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes：
  - Task 3 的 `RagEngine`；
  - Task 4 的 `VectorStore`、`sync_brand`、`sync_all`、`BrandLocks`、`index_text`。
- Produces：
  - `Retriever.retrieve(self, brand, product, job_input, run: RunContext) -> tuple[str, list[RagHit]]`。`run` 用來送出「RAG 模型準備中」，與 `ImageEngine.render_round` 的做法相同；模擬版不使用它。
  - `app.rag.retriever`：
    - `BgeRetriever(db, store, engine, locks)`；
    - 常數 `RECALL_K = 8`、`REQUERY_K = 4`、`RECALL_WHERE`、`REQUERY_WHERE`、`PREPARING = 'RAG 模型準備中'`；
    - `build_query(brand, product, job_input) -> str`。
  - runner：`PROMPT_REFS = 5`，`ragRefs` 取 Rerank 後的前 5 筆。
  - `index_text` 也接受 `RagHit`，作為 Rerank 的 passage。
  - worker：
    - `Services` 多了 `rag: RagEngine`、`vectors: VectorStore`、`locks: BrandLocks`。
    - `build_services()` 在 `rag_mode == 'bge'` 時改用 `BgeRetriever`。
    - `async warm_up_rag(services)`：在背景先執行 `prepare_files()`，再執行 `sync_all`；錯誤只記錄，不中斷 worker。

- [ ] **Step 2: 建立 `backend/tests/test_rag_retriever.py`（先寫測試）**

```python
"""BgeRetriever：FakeEncoder 與 Chroma 的 genai_test database（示範品牌的切塊，再加上幾份上傳的切塊）。"""

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.brands import load_brands
from app.contract import Brand, JobEvent, JobInput, LogLevel, LogSource, ParsedBrief, Product, RagChunk, RagHit
from app.db.session import Database
from app.rag.retriever import PREPARING, RECALL_K, REQUERY_K, BgeRetriever, build_query
from app.rag.store import VectorStore
from app.rag.sync import BrandLocks, index_text
from app.repo import catalog
from tests.fake_encoder import FakeEncoder, fake_engine
from tests.fixtures import BRANDS_DIR, load_fixture

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 21, 1, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
#: 上傳的手冊：讓品牌的一般切塊多於 8 個、法規切塊多於 4 個
UPLOAD = [
    RagChunk(id='selune#doc-x-1', source='手冊.md', section='§9 包裝', tag='general', text='外盒需印上回收標誌。'),
    RagChunk(id='selune#doc-x-2', source='手冊.md', section='§9 包裝', tag='law', text='保濕不可宣稱根治乾燥。'),
    RagChunk(id='selune#doc-x-3', source='手冊.md', section='§9 包裝', tag='law', text='廣告不得誇大保濕效果。'),
    RagChunk(id='selune#doc-x-4', source='手冊.md', section='§9 包裝', tag='law', text='療效宣稱一律禁用。'),
]
JOB_INPUT = JobInput.model_validate(load_fixture('timelines/selune-retry.json')['input'])


class RecordingRun:
    """記錄 log 的 RunContext。"""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.logs: list[tuple[LogSource, str]] = []

    async def emit(self, event: JobEvent) -> None:
        pass

    async def log(self, source: LogSource, message: str, level: LogLevel = 'info') -> None:
        self.logs.append((source, message))

    async def pace(self, ms: float) -> None:
        pass


@pytest.fixture
async def selune(session: AsyncSession) -> tuple[Brand, Product]:
    await catalog.sync_brands(session, load_brands(BRANDS_DIR)[0], NOW)
    await catalog.replace_document_chunks(session, 'selune', '手冊.md', UPLOAD, NOW)
    brand = await catalog.get_brand(session, 'selune')
    product = await catalog.get_product(session, JOB_INPUT.product_id)
    assert brand is not None and product is not None
    return brand, product


def cosine(a: str, b: str) -> float:
    return sum(x * y for x, y in zip(FakeEncoder.vector(a), FakeEncoder.vector(b), strict=True))


def test_build_query_uses_the_selling_points_or_else_the_product_features() -> None:
    brand = Brand.model_validate(load_fixture('brands.json')['brands'][0])
    product = Product.model_validate(load_fixture('brands.json')['products'][0])
    brief = ParsedBrief.model_validate({**JOB_INPUT.parsed_brief.to_json(), 'sellingPoints': ['長效保濕', '修護']})
    job_input = JOB_INPUT.model_copy(update={'parsed_brief': brief, 'brief': '秋季保濕主打'})
    assert build_query(brand, product, job_input) == f'{brand.name}｜{product.name}｜秋季保濕主打｜長效保濕、修護'
    no_points = job_input.model_copy(update={'parsed_brief': brief.model_copy(update={'selling_points': []})})
    features = '、'.join(product.features)
    assert build_query(brand, product, no_points) == f'{brand.name}｜{product.name}｜秋季保濕主打｜{features}'
    # 兩者都沒有時省略最後一段
    bare = product.model_copy(update={'features': []})
    assert build_query(brand, bare, no_points) == f'{brand.name}｜{product.name}｜秋季保濕主打'


async def test_retrieve_syncs_the_brand_and_recalls_eight_chunks_without_alt(
    selune: tuple[Brand, Product], db: Database, redis: Redis, chroma: VectorStore
) -> None:
    brand, product = selune
    encoder = FakeEncoder()
    retriever = BgeRetriever(db, chroma, fake_engine(redis, encoder), BrandLocks())
    query, hits = await retriever.retrieve(brand, product, JOB_INPUT, RecordingRun())

    assert query == build_query(brand, product, JOB_INPUT)
    # 先同步（每個切塊向量化一次），最後才是查詢
    assert len(await chroma.metadata('selune')) == len(brand.guideline_chunks)
    assert encoder.embedded[-1] == query
    assert len(encoder.embedded) == len(brand.guideline_chunks) + 1
    # 共 13 個切塊，其中 1 個是 alt：召回 8 個，依 cosine 由高到低
    assert len(hits) == RECALL_K
    assert all(not h.chunk_id.endswith('#law-alt') for h in hits)
    chunks = {c.id: c for c in brand.guideline_chunks}
    expected = sorted(
        (c for c in brand.guideline_chunks if not c.id.endswith('#law-alt')),
        key=lambda c: -cosine(query, index_text(c)),
    )
    assert [h.chunk_id for h in hits] == [c.id for c in expected[:RECALL_K]]
    for hit in hits:
        chunk = chunks[hit.chunk_id]
        assert (hit.source, hit.section, hit.tag, hit.text) == (chunk.source, chunk.section, chunk.tag, chunk.text)
        assert hit.vector_score == pytest.approx(cosine(query, index_text(chunk)), abs=1e-5)
        assert hit.rerank_score is None


async def test_rerank_scores_each_hit_and_keeps_the_vector_order_on_ties(
    db: Database, redis: Redis, chroma: VectorStore
) -> None:
    encoder = FakeEncoder()
    retriever = BgeRetriever(db, chroma, fake_engine(redis, encoder), BrandLocks())

    def hit(id_: str, text: str, score: float) -> RagHit:
        return RagHit(chunk_id=id_, source='ci.md', section='§1', tag='general', text=text, vector_score=score)

    hits = [
        hit('b#far', '咖啡產地', 0.9),
        hit('b#tie-1', '保濕', 0.8),
        hit('b#tie-2', '保濕', 0.7),
        hit('b#near', '保濕精華', 0.6),
    ]
    ranked = await retriever.rerank('保濕精華', hits)
    assert [h.chunk_id for h in ranked] == ['b#near', 'b#tie-1', 'b#tie-2', 'b#far']
    # 一次 Rerank 全部的切塊，段落為「章節：內文」；分數是 Encoder 的分數
    assert encoder.reranked == [('保濕精華', [index_text(h) for h in hits])]
    assert [h.rerank_score for h in ranked] == pytest.approx([cosine('保濕精華', index_text(h)) for h in ranked])
    assert [h.vector_score for h in ranked] == [0.6, 0.8, 0.7, 0.9]
    # 沒有切塊時不呼叫模型
    assert await retriever.rerank('保濕精華', []) == []
    assert len(encoder.reranked) == 1


async def test_requery_recalls_four_alt_or_law_chunks_with_rerank_scores(
    selune: tuple[Brand, Product], db: Database, redis: Redis, chroma: VectorStore
) -> None:
    brand, product = selune
    encoder = FakeEncoder()
    retriever = BgeRetriever(db, chroma, fake_engine(redis, encoder), BrandLocks())
    await retriever.retrieve(brand, product, JOB_INPUT, RecordingRun())
    query = '保養品 禁用詞 合規替代用語'
    hits = await retriever.requery(brand, query)
    # 候選：law、law-alt 與 3 個上傳的法規切塊
    assert len(hits) == REQUERY_K
    tags = {c.id: c.tag for c in brand.guideline_chunks}
    assert all(h.chunk_id.endswith('#law-alt') or tags[h.chunk_id] == 'law' for h in hits)
    assert all(h.rerank_score is not None for h in hits)
    assert [h.rerank_score for h in hits] == sorted((h.rerank_score or 0 for h in hits), reverse=True)
    assert encoder.embedded[-1] == query
    assert encoder.reranked[-1] == (query, [index_text(h) for h in sorted(hits, key=lambda h: -h.vector_score)])


async def test_a_brand_without_chunks_gets_no_hits(db: Database, redis: Redis, chroma: VectorStore) -> None:
    brand = Brand.model_validate({**load_fixture('brands.json')['brands'][0], 'id': 'empty', 'guidelineChunks': []})
    product = Product.model_validate(load_fixture('brands.json')['products'][0])
    retriever = BgeRetriever(db, chroma, fake_engine(redis, FakeEncoder()), BrandLocks())
    query, hits = await retriever.retrieve(brand, product, JOB_INPUT, RecordingRun())
    assert query and hits == []
    assert await retriever.rerank(query, hits) == []
    assert await retriever.requery(brand, '禁用詞') == []


async def test_logs_that_the_models_are_being_prepared_until_they_are_loaded(
    selune: tuple[Brand, Product], db: Database, redis: Redis, chroma: VectorStore
) -> None:
    brand, product = selune
    engine = fake_engine(redis, FakeEncoder())
    retriever = BgeRetriever(db, chroma, engine, BrandLocks())
    first, second = RecordingRun(), RecordingRun()
    await retriever.retrieve(brand, product, JOB_INPUT, first)
    assert first.logs == [('rag', PREPARING)]
    assert engine.loaded
    await retriever.retrieve(brand, product, JOB_INPUT, second)
    assert second.logs == []
```

```python
class EightHits(EmptyRetriever):
    """召回 8 筆，Rerank 把順序整個反過來。"""

    async def retrieve(
        self, brand: Brand, product: Product, job_input: JobInput, run: RunContext
    ) -> tuple[str, list[RagHit]]:
        hits = [
            RagHit(
                chunk_id=f'selune#c{i}', source='ci.md', section='§1', tag='general', text=f'第 {i} 條', vector_score=1
            )
            for i in range(8)
        ]
        return '查詢', hits

    async def rerank(self, query: str, hits: list[RagHit]) -> list[RagHit]:
        return [h.model_copy(update={'rerank_score': (i + 1) / 10}) for i, h in reversed(list(enumerate(hits)))]
```

```python
async def test_only_the_top_five_reranked_chunks_go_into_the_prompt(store: EventStore, db: Database) -> None:
    agent = FakeAgent()
    await run_job(JOB, db, store, ports(agent, FakeReviewer({1: {'ig-post', 'story'}}), retriever=EightHits()), speed=0)
    events = await messages(db)
    (reranked,) = [m for m in events if m['type'] == 'rag.reranked']
    # 事件送出全部 8 筆；Prompt 只用前 5 筆
    assert [h['chunkId'] for h in reranked['hits']] == [f'selune#c{i}' for i in range(7, -1, -1)]
    assert PROMPT_REFS == 5
    assert [r.rag_refs for r in agent.requests] == [[f'selune#c{i}' for i in range(7, 2, -1)]] * 2


async def failure(db: Database) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """任務失敗的原因，以及失敗時的階段（最後一個 job.status 之前的那個）。"""
    events = await messages(db)
    stages = [m['stage'] for m in events if m['type'] == 'job.status']
    (failed,) = [m for m in events if m['type'] == 'job.failed']
    assert stages[-1] == 'failed'
    return failed['reason'], stages[-2]


async def test_fails_the_job_when_chroma_is_unreachable(store: EventStore, db: Database, redis: Redis) -> None:
    retriever = BgeRetriever(db, VectorStore('http://127.0.0.1:9'), fake_engine(redis, FakeEncoder()), BrandLocks())
    await run_job(JOB, db, store, ports(FakeAgent(), FakeReviewer({}), retriever=retriever), speed=0)
    reason, stage = await failure(db)
    assert reason.startswith('向量庫（Chroma）無法連線（http://127.0.0.1:9）：')
    assert stage == 'retrieving'


async def test_fails_the_job_when_the_rag_models_cannot_be_prepared(
    store: EventStore, db: Database, redis: Redis, chroma: VectorStore, tmp_path: Path
) -> None:
    def broken(models_dir: Path) -> Encoder:
        raise RuntimeError('模型檔損毀')

    engine = RagEngine('bge', tmp_path, redis, models=[], load=broken)
    retriever = BgeRetriever(db, chroma, engine, BrandLocks())
    await run_job(JOB, db, store, ports(FakeAgent(), FakeReviewer({}), retriever=retriever), speed=0)
    reason, stage = await failure(db)
    assert (reason, stage) == ('RAG 模型準備失敗：模型檔損毀', 'retrieving')
    async with db.session() as s:
        snap = await jobs.get_job(s, JOB)
    assert snap is not None
    # 等候模型時先送出「RAG 模型準備中」
    logs = [m['message'] for m in snap.events if m['type'] == 'log' and m['source'] == 'rag']
    assert 'RAG 模型準備中' in logs
```

```python
async def test_build_services_picks_the_retriever_by_rag_mode(
    mode: RagMode, retriever: type, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:

class StateRecorder:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.states: list[object] = []

    async def __call__(self, message: dict[str, object]) -> None:
        self.states.append(message['state'])


async def test_rag_warm_up_checks_the_files_then_syncs_every_brand(
    services: Services, chroma: VectorStore, redis: Redis
) -> None:
    encoder, recorder = FakeEncoder(), StateRecorder()
    services.rag = RagEngine('bge', Path('models'), redis, models=[], load=lambda _: encoder, on_state=recorder)
    services.vectors = chroma
    await warm_up_rag(services)
    # 先回報 standby（模型檔已核對）；同步需要向量化時才載入
    assert recorder.states == ['standby', 'loading', 'ready']
    assert await chroma.brands() == ['misty-isle', 'selune']
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        assert len(await chroma.metadata('selune')) == len(await catalog.list_chunks(s, 'selune'))
```

```python
def index_text(chunk: RagChunk) -> str:
    """向量化的文字：章節名稱也帶有語意，例如「§2.1 色彩系統」。"""
    return f'{chunk.section}：{chunk.text}'
```

改成：

```python
UPSERT_BATCH = 32


def index_text(chunk: RagChunk | RagHit) -> str:
    """向量化（以及 Rerank）的文字：章節名稱也帶有語意，例如「§2.1 色彩系統」。"""
    return f'{chunk.section}：{chunk.text}'
```

- [ ] **Step 10: 建立 `backend/app/rag/retriever.py`**

```python
"""真實版的檢索（Retriever 介面）：BGE-M3 向量化查詢 → Chroma 召回 → bge-reranker-v2-m3 逐筆 Rerank。

檢索前先同步品牌（PostgreSQL → Chroma），所以不必依賴 worker 啟動時的同步是否完成。
模型或 Chroma 無法使用時丟出 RagError（中文訊息），任務以這個原因失敗。
"""

from app.contract import Brand, JobInput, Product, RagHit
from app.db.session import Database
from app.pipeline.ports import RunContext
from app.rag.engine import RagEngine
from app.rag.store import VectorHit, VectorStore, Where
from app.rag.sync import BrandLocks, index_text, sync_brand

#: 第一次檢索的召回筆數（全部 Rerank 後，前 5 筆進入 Prompt，見 runner.PROMPT_REFS）
RECALL_K = 8
#: Query Rewriting 重新檢索的召回筆數
REQUERY_K = 4
#: 第一次檢索不含合規替代用語（只在 Query Rewriting 時使用）
RECALL_WHERE: Where = {'origin': {'$ne': 'alt'}}
#: 重新檢索只找合規替代用語與法規切塊
REQUERY_WHERE: Where = {'$or': [{'origin': 'alt'}, {'tag': 'law'}]}
#: 模型還沒載入時（下載或載入中），任務在 retrieving 階段等候並送出這則 log
PREPARING = 'RAG 模型準備中'


def build_query(brand: Brand, product: Product, job_input: JobInput) -> str:
    """「品牌名｜商品名｜一句話需求｜賣點」，空的部分略過。
    賣點取 parsedBrief.sellingPoints，沒有時改用商品的 features，以「、」連接。"""
    points = job_input.parsed_brief.selling_points or product.features
    parts = [brand.name, product.name, job_input.brief, '、'.join(points)]
    return '｜'.join(p for p in parts if p)


def _hit(hit: VectorHit) -> RagHit:
    meta = hit.metadata
    # 以 model_validate 建立：tag 是 metadata 的字串，由 RagHit 檢查是否為合法的 tag
    return RagHit.model_validate(
        {
            'chunk_id': hit.id,
            'source': meta['source'],
            'section': meta['section'],
            'tag': meta['tag'],
            'text': hit.document,
            'vector_score': hit.score,
        }
    )


class BgeRetriever:
    def __init__(self, db: Database, store: VectorStore, engine: RagEngine, locks: BrandLocks) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.store = store
        self.engine = engine
        self.locks = locks

    async def retrieve(
        self, brand: Brand, product: Product, job_input: JobInput, run: RunContext
    ) -> tuple[str, list[RagHit]]:
        """同步品牌 → 向量召回 8 筆（不含 alt），依向量分數由高到低。"""
        if not self.engine.loaded:
            await run.log('rag', PREPARING)
        await sync_brand(self.db, self.store, self.engine, self.locks, brand.id)
        query = build_query(brand, product, job_input)
        return query, await self._recall(brand.id, query, RECALL_K, RECALL_WHERE)

    async def rerank(self, query: str, hits: list[RagHit]) -> list[RagHit]:
        """逐筆計算 sigmoid(logit)，由高到低排序；同分時維持原本（向量）的順序。"""
        if not hits:
            return []
        scores = await self.engine.rerank(query, [index_text(h) for h in hits])
        scored = [h.model_copy(update={'rerank_score': s}) for h, s in zip(hits, scores, strict=True)]
        return sorted(scored, key=lambda h: -(h.rerank_score or 0))

    async def requery(self, brand: Brand, query: str) -> list[RagHit]:
        """Query Rewriting：召回 4 筆合規替代用語或法規切塊並 Rerank（同一個任務開頭已同步過品牌）。"""
        return await self.rerank(query, await self._recall(brand.id, query, REQUERY_K, REQUERY_WHERE))

    async def _recall(self, brand_id: str, query: str, k: int, where: Where) -> list[RagHit]:
        embedding = await self.engine.embed(query)
        return [_hit(h) for h in await self.store.query(brand_id, embedding, k, where)]
```

```python
def build_services() -> Services:
    """RAG_MODE=bge 時檢索換成 BgeRetriever；Agent、生圖與審查仍是模擬版（Plan 6、7 換成真實版）。"""
    db = Database(settings.database_url)
    redis = Redis.from_url(settings.redis_url, decode_responses=True)
    composer = ComposerClient(settings.composer_url)
    rag = RagEngine(settings.rag_mode, settings.models_dir, redis)
    vectors = VectorStore(settings.chroma_url)
    locks = BrandLocks()
    ports = sim_ports(
        composer, first_round_fail=settings.sim_first_round_fail, force_all_fail=settings.sim_force_all_fail
    )
    if settings.rag_mode == 'bge':
        ports = replace(ports, retriever=BgeRetriever(db, vectors, rag, locks))
    cutouts = CutoutEngine(settings.cutout_model, settings.models_dir, redis)
    store = EventStore(db, redis)
    return Services(db, redis, store, composer, ports, settings.sim_speed, settings, cutouts, rag, vectors, locks)


async def warm_up_rag(services: Services) -> None:
    """worker 啟動時在背景：下載並核對 RAG 模型檔（不載入），再依序同步所有品牌到 Chroma。
    失敗只記錄（模型狀態已寫到 Redis），檢索與上傳時會再試；模擬模式只回報狀態。"""
    try:
        await services.rag.prepare_files()
        if services.rag.mode == 'bge':
            await sync_all(services.db, services.vectors, services.rag, services.locks)
    except Exception:
        logger.exception('RAG 模型準備或品牌同步失敗')


async def cutout(services: Services, product_id: str, photo_url: str) -> None:
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 取出測試用的 PDF**

PDF 是二進位檔。它的字型（微軟正黑體）會讓文字變成康熙部首字元，正好用來測試抽字的修正，所以要用草稿上的同一個檔案，不能重新產生。在 repo 根目錄執行：

```bash
git checkout plan5-draft2 -- backend/tests/data/ci-sample.pdf
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 建立 `backend/tests/test_rag_extract.py`（先寫測試）**

有密碼的 PDF 與只有圖片的 PDF，都在測試時以 pypdf 產生。

```python
"""上傳手冊的抽字：Markdown、純文字、BOM、非 UTF-8、PDF（tests/data 的中文 PDF）、有密碼與讀不到文字的 PDF。

ci-sample.pdf 由 Chromium 以微軟正黑體輸出（兩頁）。它的文字層把「月」「色」「用」等字記成康熙部首（U+2F00–），
正好用來確認抽字時會換回一般的漢字。有密碼與只有圖片的 PDF 在測試時產生。
"""

import io

import pytest
from PIL import Image
from pypdf import PdfReader, PdfWriter

from app.errors import Invalid
from app.rag.chunking import chunk_document, guess_tag
from app.rag.extract import MAX_BYTES, MAX_CHARS, NO_TEXT, check_type, document_name, extract_text
from tests.fixtures import SAMPLE_PDF as SAMPLE


def encrypted(user_password: str, owner_password: str) -> bytes:
    writer = PdfWriter(clone_from=PdfReader(SAMPLE))
    # RC4 不需要額外的加密套件
    writer.encrypt(user_password, owner_password, algorithm='RC4-128')
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    writer.write(buf)
    return buf.getvalue()


def scanned() -> bytes:
    """只有圖片、沒有文字層的 PDF（像掃描檔）。"""
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    Image.new('RGB', (200, 280), (240, 240, 240)).save(buf, 'PDF')
    return buf.getvalue()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('name', ['手冊.md', '手冊.markdown', 'notes.txt', 'CI.MD'])
def test_text_files_are_decoded_as_utf8_without_the_bom(name: str) -> None:
    text = '# 色彩\n主色為品牌紫。'
    assert extract_text(name, text.encode()) == text
    assert extract_text(name, b'\xef\xbb\xbf' + text.encode()) == text


def test_text_files_must_be_utf8() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(Invalid, match='^文字檔需為 UTF-8 編碼$'):
        extract_text('手冊.txt', '主色為品牌紫'.encode('big5'))


@pytest.mark.parametrize('name', ['手冊.docx', '手冊', 'logo.png', '.md.exe'])
def test_only_markdown_text_and_pdf_are_supported(name: str) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(Invalid, match='^只支援 .md、.txt、.pdf$'):
        check_type(name)
    with pytest.raises(Invalid, match='^只支援 .md、.txt、.pdf$'):
        extract_text(name, b'x')


def test_files_and_texts_have_size_limits() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(Invalid, match='^檔案不可超過 20 MB$'):
        extract_text('big.txt', b'a' * (MAX_BYTES + 1))
    assert len(extract_text('long.txt', b'a' * MAX_CHARS)) == MAX_CHARS
    with pytest.raises(Invalid, match=r'^文字超過 20 萬字（200,001 字），請分成幾份上傳$'):
        extract_text('long.txt', b'a' * (MAX_CHARS + 1))


def test_document_names_drop_any_path() -> None:
    assert document_name('C:\\fakepath\\晞月 CI 手冊.pdf') == '晞月 CI 手冊.pdf'
    assert document_name('a/b/手冊.md') == '手冊.md'
    assert document_name('手冊.md') == '手冊.md'


def test_pdf_pages_are_joined_by_a_blank_line_with_radicals_mapped_to_ideographs() -> None:
    pages = [page.extract_text() for page in PdfReader(SAMPLE).pages]
    # 原始的文字層：「色」是康熙部首 U+2F8A
    assert len(pages) == 2 and '\u2f8a彩系統' in pages[0]
    text = extract_text('ci-sample.pdf', SAMPLE.read_bytes())
    assert not [ch for ch in text if '\u2e80' <= ch <= '\u2fdf']
    assert text.startswith('晞月 CI 手冊\n§2.1 色彩系統\n品牌主色為晞月玫瑰 #B76E79，輔助色為月光米白 #F6EFE9。\n')
    assert '高度之淨空區。\n\n§5 廣告用語\n保養品廣告不得宣稱醫療效能。' in text
    assert text.endswith('改用「改善外觀」「專業配方」')
    # 部首看起來一樣，但切塊的標籤判斷認不得
    assert (guess_tag('主⾊佔畫⾯ 60% 以上'), guess_tag('主色佔畫面 60% 以上')) == ('general', 'color')
    # 每一頁是一段（PDF 沒有 Markdown 標題，section 是檔名）
    chunks = chunk_document('selune', 'ci-sample.pdf', text)
    assert [c.section for c in chunks] == ['ci-sample', 'ci-sample']
    assert chunks[0].text.startswith('晞月 CI 手冊 §2.1 色彩系統品牌主色為晞月玫瑰')
    assert chunks[1].text.startswith('§5 廣告用語保養品廣告不得宣稱醫療效能。')


def test_pdfs_that_need_a_password_are_refused() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(Invalid, match='^PDF 有加密保護（密碼或權限限制），請先移除保護再上傳$'):
        extract_text('locked.pdf', encrypted('secret', 'owner'))


def test_pdfs_with_only_an_owner_password_are_read() -> None:
    """只限制權限（開啟不需要密碼）的 PDF 照常抽字。"""
    assert extract_text('limited.pdf', encrypted('', 'owner')) == extract_text('ci-sample.pdf', SAMPLE.read_bytes())


def test_pdfs_without_a_text_layer_are_refused() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(Invalid, match=f'^{NO_TEXT}$'):
        extract_text('scan.pdf', scanned())
    blank = io.BytesIO()
    writer = PdfWriter()
    writer.add_blank_page(420, 595)
    writer.write(blank)
    with pytest.raises(Invalid, match=f'^{NO_TEXT}$'):
        extract_text('blank.pdf', blank.getvalue())


def test_broken_pdfs_are_refused() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(Invalid, match='^PDF 無法讀取：'):
        extract_text('broken.pdf', b'%PDF-1.4\nnot really a pdf')
    with pytest.raises(Invalid, match='^PDF 無法讀取：'):
        extract_text('empty.pdf', b'')
```

```python
async def test_ingest_streams_progress_as_ndjson(api: LiveApi) -> None:
    text = '# 色彩更新\n主色維持 #2F4A44。\n\n# Logo\n- 深色背景使用反白 Logo\n\n2026 春季素材 CTR 3.9%。'
    async with api.worker():
        lines = await ingest(api, 'misty-isle', 'ci_update.md', text.encode())
        assert [m['type'] for m in lines] == ['ingest.chunked'] + ['ingest.embedded'] * 3 + ['ingest.stored']
        assert lines[0]['chunks'] == [c.to_json() for c in chunk_document('misty-isle', 'ci_update.md', text)]
        assert lines[-1] == {'type': 'ingest.stored', 'collection': 'misty-isle_ci', 'count': 3}
        pdf = await ingest(api, 'misty-isle', 'C:\\fakepath\\ci-sample.pdf', SAMPLE_PDF.read_bytes())
        assert pdf[-1] == {'type': 'ingest.stored', 'collection': 'misty-isle_ci', 'count': 2}
        assert pdf[0]['source'] == 'ci-sample.pdf'
        empty = await ingest(api, 'misty-isle', 'empty.md', b'   ')
        assert empty == [{'error': {'code': 'invalid', 'message': '文件沒有可用的文字內容'}}]

async def upload_error(api: LiveApi, brand_id: str, files: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]:
    res = await api.http.post(f'/api/brands/{brand_id}/documents', files=files)
    assert res.headers['content-type'] == 'application/json'
    return res.status_code, res.json()['error']


async def test_ingest_rejects_bad_files_before_streaming(api: LiveApi) -> None:
    """不需要 worker：檔案的問題在放入佇列之前就回應錯誤。"""
    blank = io.BytesIO()
    writer = PdfWriter()
    writer.add_blank_page(420, 595)
    writer.write(blank)
    cases: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], int, str]] = [
        ('nope', {'file': ('a.md', b'x')}, 404, '找不到品牌：nope'),
        ('selune', {'file': ('a.docx', b'x')}, 400, '只支援 .md、.txt、.pdf'),
        ('selune', {'file': ('a.txt', '主色'.encode('big5'))}, 400, '文字檔需為 UTF-8 編碼'),
        ('selune', {'file': ('scan.pdf', blank.getvalue())}, 400, NO_TEXT),
        ('selune', {'file': ('big.md', b'a' * (MAX_BYTES + 1))}, 400, '檔案不可超過 20 MB'),
        ('selune', {'other': ('a.md', b'x')}, 400, '請求格式錯誤：body.file：Field required'),
    ]
    for brand_id, files, status, message in cases:
        code = 'not_found' if status == 404 else 'invalid'
        assert await upload_error(api, brand_id, files) == (status, {'code': code, 'message': message})
    assert await api.services.queues.tasks.count('queued') == 0


async def test_ingest_times_out_only_after_a_pause_in_progress(api: LiveApi, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    """閒置逾時：持續有進度時，總時間超過逾時也照常完成；沒有進度（沒有 worker）時才回報逾時。"""
    monkeypatch.setattr(catalog_api, 'INGEST_IDLE_TIMEOUT', 1)
    publishing: list[asyncio.Task[None]] = []

    async def slow_worker(function: str, /, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        async def publish() -> None:
            channel = ingest_channel(kwargs['task_id'])
            for i in range(6):
                await asyncio.sleep(0.4)
                event = {'type': 'ingest.embedded', 'chunkId': 'x', 'index': i, 'total': 6, 'dims': 1, 'preview': []}
                await api.services.redis.publish(channel, json.dumps(event))
            stored = {'type': 'ingest.stored', 'collection': 'selune_ci', 'count': 6}
            await api.services.redis.publish(channel, json.dumps(stored))

        publishing.append(asyncio.create_task(publish()))

    monkeypatch.setattr(api.services.queues, 'enqueue_task', slow_worker)
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    started = loop.time()
    lines = await ingest(api, 'selune', 'a.md', '# 色彩\n主色'.encode())
    assert loop.time() - started > 2
    assert [m['type'] for m in lines] == ['ingest.embedded'] * 6 + ['ingest.stored']
    await asyncio.gather(*publishing)

    async def no_worker(function: str, /, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        pass

    monkeypatch.setattr(api.services.queues, 'enqueue_task', no_worker)
    message = '文件處理逾時：1 秒沒有任何進度，請確認 worker 正在執行'
    assert await ingest(api, 'selune', 'a.md', '# 色彩\n主色'.encode()) == [
        {'error': {'code': 'unavailable', 'message': message}}
    ]
```

```python
DOC = '# 色彩更新\n主色維持 #2F4A44。\n\n# Logo\n- 深色背景使用反白 Logo\n\n2026 春季素材 CTR 3.9%。'

async def uploads(services: Services, brand_id: str = 'misty-isle') -> list[RagChunk]:
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        return [c for c, origin in await catalog.list_chunks(s, brand_id) if origin == 'upload']


def embedded(chunks: list[RagChunk], previews: list[list[float]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [
        IngestEmbeddedEvent(
            type='ingest.embedded', chunk_id=c.id, index=i, total=len(chunks), dims=EMBED_DIMS, preview=p
        ).to_json()
        for i, (c, p) in enumerate(zip(chunks, previews, strict=True))
    ]

async def test_sim_ingest_chunks_in_python_with_simulated_vectors(services: Services) -> None:
    messages = await collect_ingest(services, 't1', 'ci_update.md', DOC)
    chunks = chunk_document('misty-isle', 'ci_update.md', DOC)
    assert len(chunks) == 3
    assert messages == [

        *embedded(chunks, [embed_preview(c.text) for c in chunks]),
        STORED,
    ]

async def test_bge_ingest_embeds_each_chunk_then_writes_postgres_and_chroma(
    services: Services, bge: FakeEncoder, chroma: VectorStore
) -> None:
    messages = await collect_ingest(services, 't1', 'ci_update.md', DOC)
    chunks = chunk_document('misty-isle', 'ci_update.md', DOC)
    # 逐塊向量化「章節：內文」；進度是真實向量的前 6 維（四捨五入到小數 3 位）
    assert bge.embedded == [index_text(c) for c in chunks]
    previews = [vector_preview(FakeEncoder.vector(index_text(c))) for c in chunks]
    assert messages == [
        IngestChunkedEvent(type='ingest.chunked', source='ci_update.md', chunks=chunks).to_json(),
        *embedded(chunks, previews),
        STORED,
    ]
    assert await uploads(services) == chunks
    assert await chroma.metadata('misty-isle') == {c.id: chunk_metadata('misty-isle', c, 'upload') for c in chunks}

    # 同名文件重新上傳時取代舊的切塊（切塊變少時，多的要刪掉）；其他文件不受影響
    other = '# 字型\n標題使用 Noto Serif TC。'
    shorter = '# 色彩更新\n主色改為 #1F3A34。'
    await collect_ingest(services, 't2', 'other.md', other)
    assert (await collect_ingest(services, 't3', 'ci_update.md', shorter))[-1]['type'] == 'ingest.stored'
    expected = chunk_document('misty-isle', 'other.md', other) + chunk_document('misty-isle', 'ci_update.md', shorter)
    assert await uploads(services) == expected
    assert await chroma.metadata('misty-isle') == {c.id: chunk_metadata('misty-isle', c, 'upload') for c in expected}


async def test_bge_ingest_embeds_outside_the_brand_lock_but_writes_inside_it(
    services: Services, bge: FakeEncoder
) -> None:
    async with services.locks('misty-isle'):
        task = asyncio.create_task(collect_ingest(services, 't1', 'ci_update.md', DOC))
        for _ in range(50):
            if len(bge.embedded) == 3:
                break
            await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        assert len(bge.embedded) == 3
        assert await uploads(services) == []
        assert not task.done()
    assert (await task)[-1] == STORED
    assert len(await uploads(services)) == 3
```

- [ ] **Step 8: 建立 `backend/app/rag/extract.py`**

```python
"""上傳的品牌手冊 → 文字：Markdown 與純文字以 UTF-8 解碼（可有 BOM），PDF 以 pypdf 逐頁抽出文字層（頁與頁之間空一行）。

api 在開始串流之前呼叫（在執行緒裡）；檔案有問題時丟出 Invalid（400），訊息為繁體中文。之後只把文字放進佇列。
"""

import io
import re
import unicodedata
from pathlib import PurePosixPath

from pypdf import PasswordType, PdfReader

from app.errors import Invalid

TEXT_TYPES = frozenset({'.md', '.markdown', '.txt'})
PDF_TYPE = '.pdf'
#: 單檔上限（位元組）
MAX_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024
#: 抽出的文字上限（字數，以 code point 計算）：約 1,100 個以上的切塊，向量化需要幾分鐘
MAX_CHARS = 200_000

NO_TEXT = 'PDF 沒有可讀取的文字（可能是掃描檔），請先轉成文字'

#: 康熙部首（U+2F00–）與 CJK 部首補充（U+2E80–）→ 一般的漢字，例如「⽉」→「月」。
#: Windows 的微軟正黑體等字型把部首與漢字對應到同一個字形，Chromium、Office 輸出 PDF 時，
#: 文字層常記成部首：看起來一樣，但切塊的標籤判斷（「色彩」）與向量化都會認不得
_RADICALS = {
    cp: unicodedata.normalize('NFKC', chr(cp))
    for cp in range(0x2E80, 0x2FE0)
    if unicodedata.normalize('NFKC', chr(cp)) != chr(cp)
}


def document_name(filename: str) -> str:
    """上傳的檔名（部分瀏覽器會帶完整路徑，只取最後一段）。"""
    return re.split(r'[\\/]', filename)[-1].strip()


def check_type(name: str) -> str:
    """副檔名（小寫）；不支援時丟出 Invalid。"""
    suffix = PurePosixPath(name).suffix.lower()
    if suffix not in TEXT_TYPES and suffix != PDF_TYPE:
        raise Invalid('只支援 .md、.txt、.pdf')
    return suffix


def extract_text(name: str, data: bytes) -> str:
    suffix = check_type(name)
    if len(data) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise Invalid(f'檔案不可超過 {MAX_BYTES // 1024 // 1024} MB')
    text = _pdf_text(data) if suffix == PDF_TYPE else _decode(data)
    if len(text) > MAX_CHARS:
        raise Invalid(f'文字超過 {MAX_CHARS // 10_000} 萬字（{len(text):,} 字），請分成幾份上傳')
    return text


def _decode(data: bytes) -> str:
    try:
        return data.decode('utf-8-sig')
    except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
        raise Invalid('文字檔需為 UTF-8 編碼') from error


def _pdf_text(data: bytes) -> str:
    try:
        reader = PdfReader(io.BytesIO(data))
        # 只設定權限（擁有者密碼）的 PDF 可以用空白密碼開啟；需要密碼才能開啟的拒絕。
        # AES 加密的 PDF 需要 cryptography 套件，沒有安裝時解密會出錯，同樣視為有加密保護
        if reader.is_encrypted and _decrypt(reader) == PasswordType.NOT_DECRYPTED:
            raise Invalid('PDF 有加密保護（密碼或權限限制），請先移除保護再上傳')
        pages = [page.extract_text() for page in reader.pages]
    except Invalid:
        raise
    except Exception as error:
        raise Invalid(f'PDF 無法讀取：{error}') from error
    text = '\n\n'.join(pages).translate(_RADICALS)
    if not text.strip():
        raise Invalid(NO_TEXT)
    return text


def _decrypt(reader: PdfReader) -> PasswordType:
    try:
        return reader.decrypt('')
    except Exception:
        return PasswordType.NOT_DECRYPTED
```

```python
def embed_preview(text: str, n: int = 6) -> list[float]:
    """模擬向量的前幾維（同前端 embedPreview）：由文字的雜湊決定，四捨五入到小數 3 位。"""
    rng = Mulberry32(hash_string(text))
    return [_js_round((rng() * 2 - 1) * 1000) / 1000 for _ in range(n)]
```

```python
    return floor + 1 if x - floor >= 0.5 else floor


def vector_preview(vector: Sequence[float], n: int = 6) -> list[float]:
    """向量的前幾維，四捨五入到小數 3 位（同 JS 的 Math.round(x * 1000) / 1000）：上傳進度顯示的向量值。"""
    return [_js_round(x * 1000) / 1000 for x in vector[:n]]


def embed_preview(text: str, n: int = 6) -> list[float]:
    """模擬向量的前幾維（同前端 embedPreview）：由文字的雜湊決定，四捨五入到小數 3 位。"""
    rng = Mulberry32(hash_string(text))
    return vector_preview([rng() * 2 - 1 for _ in range(n)], n)
```

```python
async def _ingest_lines(services: ApiServices, task_id: str, brand_id: str, doc: BrandDocument) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    """先訂閱再放入佇列，才不會漏掉 worker 發布的訊息；收到 ingest.stored 或錯誤時結束。"""
    pubsub = services.redis.pubsub()
    await pubsub.subscribe(ingest_channel(task_id))
    try:
        await services.queues.enqueue_task('ingest', task_id=task_id, brand_id=brand_id, name=doc.name, text=doc.text)
        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        deadline = loop.time() + INGEST_TIMEOUT
        while loop.time() < deadline:
            raw = await pubsub.get_message(ignore_subscribe_messages=True, timeout=1.0)
            if raw is None:
                continue
            yield raw['data'] + '\n'
            message = json.loads(raw['data'])
            if 'error' in message or message['type'] == 'ingest.stored':
                return
        timeout = {'code': 'unavailable', 'message': '文件處理逾時：請確認 worker 正在執行'}
        yield json.dumps({'error': timeout}, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n'
    finally:
        await pubsub.unsubscribe()
        await pubsub.aclose()  # type: ignore[no-untyped-call]
```

```python
async def _ingest_lines(services: ApiServices, task_id: str, brand_id: str, name: str, text: str) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    """先訂閱再放入佇列，才不會漏掉 worker 發布的訊息；收到 ingest.stored 或錯誤時結束。
    每收到一則進度就重新計算逾時：只有連續 INGEST_IDLE_TIMEOUT 秒沒有進度才算逾時。"""
    pubsub = services.redis.pubsub()
    await pubsub.subscribe(ingest_channel(task_id))
    try:
        await services.queues.enqueue_task('ingest', task_id=task_id, brand_id=brand_id, name=name, text=text)
        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        deadline = loop.time() + INGEST_IDLE_TIMEOUT
        while loop.time() < deadline:
            raw = await pubsub.get_message(ignore_subscribe_messages=True, timeout=1.0)
            if raw is None:
                continue
            deadline = loop.time() + INGEST_IDLE_TIMEOUT
            yield raw['data'] + '\n'
            message = json.loads(raw['data'])
            if 'error' in message or message['type'] == 'ingest.stored':
                return
        message = f'文件處理逾時：{INGEST_IDLE_TIMEOUT} 秒沒有任何進度，請確認 worker 正在執行'
        yield json.dumps({'error': {'code': 'unavailable', 'message': message}}, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n'
    finally:
        await pubsub.unsubscribe()
        await pubsub.aclose()  # type: ignore[no-untyped-call]
```

```python
    """上傳品牌文件：切塊 → 向量化 → 寫入知識庫，進度以 NDJSON 串流回報。"""
```

```python
    response_class=StreamingResponse,

async def ingest_document(brand_id: str, file: UploadFile, services: Services) -> StreamingResponse:
    """上傳品牌手冊（multipart 的 file 欄位：.md、.txt、.pdf）：切塊 → 向量化 → 寫入知識庫，進度以 NDJSON 串流回報。

    檔案的問題（格式、大小、編碼、PDF 加密或沒有文字）在開始串流之前就回應錯誤；之後只把文字放進佇列，不傳原檔。
    """
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        if await catalog.get_brand(s, brand_id) is None:
            raise NotFound(f'找不到品牌：{brand_id}')
    name = document_name(file.filename or '')
    check_type(name)
    data = await file.read(MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES + 1)
    text = await asyncio.to_thread(extract_text, name, data)
    lines = _ingest_lines(services, secrets.token_hex(8), brand_id, name, text)
    # nginx 預設會先緩衝回應；關掉才能一行一行送到前端
    return StreamingResponse(lines, media_type='application/x-ndjson', headers={'X-Accel-Buffering': 'no'})
```

```python
def _embedded(chunks: list[RagChunk], i: int, dims: int, preview: list[float]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    event = IngestEmbeddedEvent(
        type='ingest.embedded', chunk_id=chunks[i].id, index=i, total=len(chunks), dims=dims, preview=preview
    )
    return event.to_json()


async def _ingest_bge(services: Services, brand_id: str, name: str, chunks: list[RagChunk], send: Send) -> None:
    """逐塊向量化（進度顯示真實向量的前 6 維）；完成後在品牌鎖內先寫 PostgreSQL，再寫 Chroma：
    先刪除同一個 source 的上傳切塊，再 upsert。Chroma 寫入失敗時 PostgreSQL 的資料仍在，下一次同步會補上。"""
    await send(IngestChunkedEvent(type='ingest.chunked', source=name, chunks=chunks).to_json())
    records: list[VectorRecord] = []
    for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
        record = await vector_record(services.rag, brand_id, chunk, 'upload')
        records.append(record)
        await send(_embedded(chunks, i, len(record.embedding), vector_preview(record.embedding)))
    async with services.locks(brand_id):
        async with services.db.session() as s:
            await catalog.replace_document_chunks(s, brand_id, name, chunks, utc_now())
        await services.vectors.delete(brand_id, where={'$and': [{'source': name}, {'origin': 'upload'}]})
        for batch in batched(records, UPSERT_BATCH, strict=False):
            await services.vectors.upsert(brand_id, batch)


async def _ingest_sim(services: Services, brand_id: str, name: str, chunks: list[RagChunk], send: Send) -> None:
    """模擬版：向量是雜湊產生的假數字，依 1× 時間軸等待；只寫入 PostgreSQL。"""
    await services.pace(400)
    await send(IngestChunkedEvent(type='ingest.chunked', source=name, chunks=chunks).to_json())
    for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
        await services.pace(250)
        await send(_embedded(chunks, i, EMBED_DIMS, embed_preview(chunk.text)))
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        await catalog.replace_document_chunks(s, brand_id, name, chunks, utc_now())
    await services.pace(300)


async def write_back(services: Services, history_id: str) -> bool:
```

```nginx
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;

        proxy_read_timeout 150s;
```

### Task 7：前端改為上傳檔案（支援 PDF）

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - 型別與 API 客戶端：`web/src/types/contract.ts`、`web/src/lib/api/client.ts`、`web/src/lib/api/http/client.ts`、`web/src/lib/api/mock/client.ts`
  - 切塊：`web/src/lib/rag/chunk.ts`、`web/src/test/chunk-samples.ts`
  - 元件：`web/src/components/brands/doc-ingest.tsx`
  - live E2E：`web/e2e-live/live.spec.ts`、`web/e2e-live/stack.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/api/http/client.test.ts`、`web/src/lib/api/mock/client.test.ts`、`web/e2e-live/live.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes：Task 6 的 multipart API（欄位 `file`）。
- Produces：
  - `ApiClient.ingestBrandDocument(brandId: string, file: File, onEvent): Promise<RagChunk[]>`：
    - `HttpApiClient` 以 `FormData` 上傳，不設定 content-type。串流開始前的 400 轉成 `ApiError`。
    - `MockApiClient` 以 `file.text()` 讀取 `.md`、`.markdown`、`.txt`；其他格式回報「示範模式只能讀取 .md、.txt」。
  - 合約移除 `BrandDocument`；切塊用的 `{ name, text }` 改名為 `TextDocument`，從 `web/src/lib/rag/chunk.ts` 匯出。`ChunkSample` 改為延伸它。
  - live E2E 另外上傳 `backend/tests/data/ci-sample.pdf`：2 個切塊，章節為 `ci-sample`。

改成：

```ts
    { type: 'ingest.stored', collection: 'selune_ci', count: 1 },
  ];

  const file = (name = 'a.md', text = '# 色彩\n主色') => new File([text], name, { type: 'text/markdown' });

  it('uploads the file as multipart, reports each NDJSON line and returns the new chunks', async () => {
    const { api, fetch, call } = setup(() => ndjson(progress));
    const seen: IngestEvent[] = [];
    expect(await api.ingestBrandDocument('selune', file(), (e) => seen.push(e))).toEqual([chunk]);
    expect(seen).toEqual(progress);
    expect([call(0).method, call(0).url]).toEqual(['POST', 'http://api.test/api/brands/selune/documents']);
    const form = call(0).body as FormData;
    expect([...form.keys()]).toEqual(['file']);
    const sent = form.get('file') as File;
    expect([sent.name, await sent.text()]).toEqual(['a.md', '# 色彩\n主色']);
    // 不可自己設定 content-type：multipart 的 boundary 由 fetch 產生
    expect(fetch.mock.calls[0][1]?.headers).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('throws the 400 sent before streaming, the error line, or when the stream ends early', async () => {
    const NO_TEXT = 'PDF 沒有可讀取的文字（可能是掃描檔），請先轉成文字';
    let reply = () => Response.json({ error: { code: 'invalid', message: NO_TEXT } }, { status: 400 });
    const { api } = setup(() => reply());
    const pdf = new File(['%PDF-1.7'], 'scan.pdf', { type: 'application/pdf' });
    await expect(api.ingestBrandDocument('selune', pdf, () => {})).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'invalid', status: 400, message: NO_TEXT });
    reply = () => ndjson([{ error: { code: 'unavailable', message: '向量庫（Chroma）無法連線：連線被拒' } }]);
    await expect(api.ingestBrandDocument('selune', file(), () => {})).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'unavailable' });
    reply = () => ndjson(progress.slice(0, 2));
    await expect(api.ingestBrandDocument('selune', file(), () => {})).rejects.toThrow('文件上傳的進度中斷');
  });
});
```

```ts
export function chunkDocument(brandId: string, doc: BrandDocument, { maxChars = 180 } = {}): RagChunk[] {
```

### Task 8：選定的海報回寫到知識庫

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/rag/history.py`、`web/src/lib/rag/history.ts`
- Modify:
  - 後端：`backend/app/repo/catalog.py`、`backend/app/repo/history.py`、`backend/app/queues.py`、`backend/app/worker.py`、`backend/app/api/jobs.py`
  - 前端：`web/src/lib/api/mock/client.ts`
- Test: `backend/tests/fixtures.py`、`backend/tests/test_rag_history.py`、`backend/tests/test_api_jobs.py`、`backend/tests/test_worker.py`、`web/src/lib/rag/history.test.ts`、`web/src/lib/api/mock/client.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes：
  - Task 4 的 `vector_record`、`BrandLocks`；
  - Task 5 的 `Services.rag`、`Services.vectors`、`Services.locks`。
- Produces：
  - `app.rag.history`：
    - `history_chunk(entry: HistoryEntry, product_name: str | None, tz: tzinfo) -> RagChunk`；
    - `history_text(entry, label, product_name) -> str`；
    - `PROMPT_CHARS = 120`。
    - 文字以商品名稱開頭，例如「極光玻尿酸修護精華 30ml・IG 貼文海報，審查 99 分。標題：…」。商品已刪除時省略名稱。
  - `app.repo.catalog.upsert_chunk(session, brand_id, chunk, origin, now)`：以 id upsert，保留第一次的 `created_at`。
  - `app.repo.history.get_history(session, history_id) -> HistoryEntry | None`。
  - `app.queues.TaskFailed(RuntimeError)`：`run_task` 在 SAQ 回報失敗或中止時丟出。
  - worker 的 `write_back` 依序執行：
    1. upsert 到 PostgreSQL；
    2. 真實版在品牌鎖外向量化，鎖內寫入 Chroma；
    3. `mark_written`。
  - API 的 `select_render` 遇到 `TaskFailed` 時記錄警告，回傳 201 與 `writtenToRag: false`。
  - TS `historyChunk(entry: HistoryEntry, productName: string | null): RagChunk`：文字與 Python 版逐字相同。mock 把這些切塊存在 `historyChunks`，排在上傳的切塊之後。
  - 測試輔助：`tests.fixtures.selected_history()`。

```python
def selected_history() -> HistoryEntry:
    """選定 selune-retry 任務的 IG 貼文最佳候選（第 2 輪 v0，審查 99 分）。
    web/src/lib/rag/history.test.ts 以同一筆歷史紀錄比對同一段回寫文字。"""
    data = load_fixture('timelines/selune-retry.json')

        {

            'brandId': 'selune',

            'input': data['input'],

            'copy': render['spec']['copy'],
            'selectedAt': '2026-10-21T16:30:00.000Z',
            'favorite': False,
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 建立 `backend/tests/test_rag_history.py`（先寫測試）**

```python
"""歷史回寫切塊。web/src/lib/rag/history.test.ts 以同一筆歷史紀錄比對同一段文字（mock 的知識庫與後端一致）。"""

from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from app.contract import HistoryEntry, RagChunk
from app.rag.history import history_chunk
from tests.fixtures import selected_history

TAIPEI = ZoneInfo('Asia/Taipei')
#: selected_history 的商品（prod_selune_serum）
PRODUCT = '極光玻尿酸修護精華 30ml'

#: 與 web/src/lib/rag/history.test.ts 的 TEXT 相同
TEXT = (
    '極光玻尿酸修護精華 30ml・IG 貼文海報，審查 99 分。標題：一夜溫柔修護／醒來透亮發光。'
    '副標：7天感受透亮光采・專業修護配方*。賣點：5重玻尿酸、神經醯胺、無香精。'
    '正向提示詞：4:5 commercial product photography, the provided product photo on a styled surface, '
    'serene luxury skincare, crescent moo…'
)


def edited(entry: HistoryEntry, *, score: int | None, prompt: str, **copy: object) -> HistoryEntry:
    spec = entry.render.spec.model_copy(update={'positive_prompt': prompt})
    render = entry.render.model_copy(update={'score': score, 'spec': spec})
    return entry.model_copy(update={'render': render, 'copy_': entry.copy_.model_copy(update=copy)})


def test_builds_the_chunk_from_the_selected_poster() -> None:
    assert history_chunk(selected_history(), PRODUCT, TAIPEI) == RagChunk(
        id='selune#hist-hist_3fa2c1d0e9b8',
        source='history/job_20261007_1000_a1b2',
        # 2026-10-21T16:30Z 是台灣時間 10 月 22 日 00:30
        section='IG 貼文・2026-10-22',
        tag='history',
        text=TEXT,
    )


def test_the_section_date_follows_the_time_zone() -> None:
    assert history_chunk(selected_history(), PRODUCT, ZoneInfo('UTC')).section == 'IG 貼文・2026-10-21'


def test_leaves_out_the_product_name_when_the_product_was_deleted() -> None:
    assert history_chunk(selected_history(), None, TAIPEI).text == TEXT.removeprefix(f'{PRODUCT}・')


def test_leaves_out_empty_fields_and_cuts_the_prompt_at_120_characters() -> None:
    entry = selected_history().model_copy(update={'template_id': 'story'})
    bare = edited(entry, score=None, prompt='a' * 120, headline=('只有一行', ''), subhead='', features=[])
    expected = f'商品・限時動態海報，未經審查評分。標題：只有一行。正向提示詞：{"a" * 120}'
    assert history_chunk(bare, '商品', TAIPEI).text == expected
    # 以 code point 計算（與 TypeScript 相同，不是 UTF-16）：第 121 個字起省略
    long = edited(entry, score=80, prompt='𠀀' * 121, headline=('', ''), subhead='副標', features=['A'])
    expected = f'限時動態海報，審查 80 分。副標：副標。賣點：A。正向提示詞：{"𠀀" * 120}…'
    assert history_chunk(long, None, TAIPEI).text == expected
```

```python
async def test_select_answers_written_to_rag_false_when_the_write_back_fails(
    api: LiveApi, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture
) -> None:
    async def broken(services: Any, history_id: str) -> bool:
        raise RuntimeError('向量庫（Chroma）無法連線')

    monkeypatch.setattr('app.worker.write_back', broken)
    async with api.worker():
        job_id = (await api.http.post('/api/jobs', json=job_input())).json()['jobId']
        messages = await stream(api, job_id)
        render_id = (await api.http.get(f'/api/jobs/{job_id}')).json()['summary']['bestRenderIds']['story']
        rendered = [r for m in messages if m['type'] == 'render.completed' for r in m['renders']]
        copy = next(r for r in rendered if r['id'] == render_id)['spec']['copy']
        res = await api.http.post(f'/api/jobs/{job_id}/select', json={'renderId': render_id, 'copy': copy})
    # 歷史紀錄已經建立，所以不回 500；重送也不會重複建立
    assert res.status_code == 201
    entry = res.json()
    assert entry['writtenToRag'] is False
    assert (await api.http.get('/api/history', params={'brandId': 'selune'})).json() == [entry]
    assert f'歷史回寫失敗：history_id={entry["id"]}' in caplog.text
```

```python
async def selected(services: Services) -> HistoryEntry:
    """寫入歷史紀錄（選定 selune-retry 的 IG 貼文）；history 參照 jobs，所以先建立任務。"""
    entry = selected_history()
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        await jobs.create_job(s, entry.job_id, entry.input, NOW)
        await history.add_history(s, entry)
    return entry


async def chunks_by_origin(services: Services, brand_id: str = 'selune') -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        return [(c.id, origin) for c, origin in await catalog.list_chunks(s, brand_id)]


async def history_chunks(services: Services) -> list[RagChunk]:
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        return [c for c, origin in await catalog.list_chunks(s, 'selune') if origin == 'history']


async def written(services: Services, history_id: str) -> bool:
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        entry = await history.get_history(s, history_id)
    assert entry is not None
    return entry.written_to_rag


async def test_sim_write_back_upserts_the_history_chunk_into_postgres_only(
    services: Services, chroma: VectorStore
) -> None:
    services.vectors = chroma
    entry = await selected(services)
    chunk = history_chunk(entry, '極光玻尿酸修護精華 30ml', ZoneInfo('Asia/Taipei'))
    assert await write_back(services, entry.id) is True
    assert await history_chunks(services) == [chunk]
    assert await written(services, entry.id) is True
    # 列在知識庫的最後（設定檔、合規替代用語、上傳的手冊之後）；重跑以 id upsert，不會重複
    assert await write_back(services, entry.id) is True
    assert (await chunks_by_origin(services))[-1] == (chunk.id, 'history')
    assert await history_chunks(services) == [chunk]
    # 模擬模式不連 Chroma
    assert await chroma.brands() == []
    assert await write_back(services, 'hist_nope') is False


async def test_write_back_leaves_out_the_name_of_a_deleted_product(services: Services) -> None:
    entry = await selected(services)
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        assert await catalog.delete_product(s, entry.product_id)
    assert await write_back(services, entry.id) is True
    [chunk] = await history_chunks(services)
    assert chunk == history_chunk(entry, None, ZoneInfo('Asia/Taipei'))
    assert chunk.text.startswith('IG 貼文海報，審查 99 分。')


async def test_bge_write_back_embeds_the_chunk_into_chroma_where_retrieval_finds_it(
    services: Services, bge: FakeEncoder, chroma: VectorStore
) -> None:
    entry = await selected(services)
    await sync_brand(services.db, chroma, services.rag, services.locks, 'selune')
    bge.embedded.clear()
    chunk = history_chunk(entry, '極光玻尿酸修護精華 30ml', ZoneInfo('Asia/Taipei'))
    assert await write_back(services, entry.id) is True
    assert bge.embedded == [index_text(chunk)]
    assert (await chroma.metadata('selune'))[chunk.id] == chunk_metadata('selune', chunk, 'history')
    assert await history_chunks(services) == [chunk]
    assert await written(services, entry.id) is True
    # 寫入的內容與同步要的相同：之後的同步不必重新向量化
    assert await sync_brand(services.db, chroma, services.rag, services.locks, 'selune') == SyncResult(0, 0)
    assert len(bge.embedded) == 1
    hits = await chroma.query('selune', FakeEncoder.vector(index_text(chunk)), 1)
    assert [h.id for h in hits] == [chunk.id]


async def test_bge_write_back_writes_postgres_and_embeds_outside_the_brand_lock(
    services: Services, bge: FakeEncoder, chroma: VectorStore
) -> None:
    entry = await selected(services)
    async with services.locks('selune'):
        task = asyncio.create_task(write_back(services, entry.id))
        for _ in range(50):
            if bge.embedded:
                break
            await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        assert len(bge.embedded) == 1
        assert len(await history_chunks(services)) == 1
        assert not task.done()
        assert await chroma.metadata('selune') == {}
    assert await task is True
    assert list(await chroma.metadata('selune')) == [f'selune#hist-{entry.id}']


async def test_bge_write_back_fails_when_chroma_is_down_but_keeps_postgres(
    services: Services, bge: FakeEncoder
) -> None:
    services.vectors = VectorStore('http://127.0.0.1:9')
    entry = await selected(services)
    with pytest.raises(Unavailable, match=r'^向量庫（Chroma）無法連線'):
        await write_back(services, entry.id)
    # PostgreSQL 為準：下一次同步會補上 Chroma；writtenToRag 維持 false
    assert len(await history_chunks(services)) == 1
    assert await written(services, entry.id) is False


async def test_run_task_raises_task_failed_when_the_task_fails_or_is_aborted(
    services: Services, queues: Queues, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    async def broken(services: Services, history_id: str) -> bool:
        raise RuntimeError('boom')

    monkeypatch.setattr('app.worker.write_back', broken)
    async with running(services, queues):
        with pytest.raises(TaskFailed, match='背景工作 write_back 失敗'):
            await queues.run_task('write_back', wait=5, history_id='hist_x')
    # 沒有 worker：排隊中的工作被中止
    waiting = asyncio.create_task(queues.run_task('write_back', wait=5, history_id='hist_y'))
    for _ in range(50):
        queued = [job async for job in queues.tasks.iter_jobs(statuses=[Status.QUEUED])]
        if queued:
            break
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
    await queues.tasks.abort(queued[0], 'stop')
    with pytest.raises(TaskFailed, match='stop'):
        await waiting
```

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { HistoryEntry, JobEvent, JobInput } from '@/types/contract';
import { FIXTURES_DIR } from '@/test/golden';
import { historyChunk } from './history';

/** selectedHistory 的商品（prod_selune_serum） */
const PRODUCT = '極光玻尿酸修護精華 30ml';

/** 與 backend/tests/test_rag_history.py 的 TEXT 相同：兩邊以同一筆歷史紀錄比對同一段文字 */
const TEXT =
  '極光玻尿酸修護精華 30ml・IG 貼文海報，審查 99 分。標題：一夜溫柔修護／醒來透亮發光。'
  + '副標：7天感受透亮光采・專業修護配方*。賣點：5重玻尿酸、神經醯胺、無香精。'
  + '正向提示詞：4:5 commercial product photography, the provided product photo on a styled surface, '
  + 'serene luxury skincare, crescent moo…';

/** 選定 selune-retry 任務的 IG 貼文最佳候選（第 2 輪 v0，審查 99 分）；與後端 tests/fixtures.py 的 selected_history 相同 */
function selectedHistory(): HistoryEntry {
  const timeline = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('timelines/selune-retry.json', FIXTURES_DIR), 'utf8')) as {
    jobId: string; input: JobInput; events: { event: JobEvent }[];
  };
  const events = timeline.events.map((x) => x.event);
  const best = events.flatMap((e) => (e.type === 'job.completed' ? [e.bestRenderIds['ig-post']] : []))[0];
  const render = events.flatMap((e) => (e.type === 'render.completed' ? e.renders : [])).find((r) => r.id === best)!;
  return {
    id: 'hist_3fa2c1d0e9b8', jobId: timeline.jobId, brandId: 'selune', productId: timeline.input.productId, input: timeline.input,
    templateId: 'ig-post', render: { ...render, score: 99, passed: true }, copy: render.spec.copy,
    selectedAt: '2026-10-21T16:30:00.000Z', favorite: false, writtenToRag: false,
  };
}

describe('historyChunk', () => {
  it('builds the same chunk as the backend from the selected poster', () => {
    expect(historyChunk(selectedHistory(), PRODUCT)).toEqual({
      id: 'selune#hist-hist_3fa2c1d0e9b8',
      source: 'history/job_20261007_1000_a1b2',
      // 2026-10-21T16:30Z 是台灣時間 10 月 22 日 00:30（與執行測試的電腦時區無關）
      section: 'IG 貼文・2026-10-22',
      tag: 'history',
      text: TEXT,
    });
  });

  it('leaves out the product name when the product was deleted', () => {
    expect(historyChunk(selectedHistory(), null).text).toBe(TEXT.replace(`${PRODUCT}・`, ''));
  });

  it('leaves out empty fields and cuts the prompt at 120 code points (not UTF-16 units)', () => {
    const entry = selectedHistory();
    const edited = (score: number | null, positivePrompt: string, copy: Partial<HistoryEntry['copy']>): HistoryEntry => ({
      ...entry, templateId: 'story',
      render: { ...entry.render, score, spec: { ...entry.render.spec, positivePrompt } },
      copy: { ...entry.copy, ...copy },
    });
    const bare = edited(null, 'a'.repeat(120), { headline: ['只有一行', ''], subhead: '', features: [] });
    expect(historyChunk(bare, '商品').text).toBe(`商品・限時動態海報，未經審查評分。標題：只有一行。正向提示詞：${'a'.repeat(120)}`);
    const long = edited(80, '𠀀'.repeat(121), { headline: ['', ''], subhead: '副標', features: ['A'] });
    expect(historyChunk(long, null).text).toBe(`限時動態海報，審查 80 分。副標：副標。賣點：A。正向提示詞：${'𠀀'.repeat(120)}…`);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 9: 建立 `backend/app/rag/history.py`**

```python
"""選定的海報 → 歷史回寫切塊：之後的任務檢索得到過去選定的海報。

web/src/lib/rag/history.ts 以相同的格式產生 mock 的切塊，修改時兩邊一起改（兩邊的測試比對同一段文字）。
"""

from datetime import tzinfo

from app.contract import HistoryEntry, RagChunk
from app.core.clock import parse_iso
from app.templates import get_template

#: 正向提示詞只取前面這麼多字（以 code point 計算），超過時加上「…」
PROMPT_CHARS = 120


def history_chunk(entry: HistoryEntry, product_name: str | None, tz: tzinfo) -> RagChunk:
    """id 為 {brandId}#hist-{historyId}（以 id upsert，重跑不會重複）；section 為「模板名稱・選定日期」，
    日期以 tz 計算。product_name 為回寫時的商品名稱，商品已刪除時為 None（文字省略商品名稱）。"""
    label = get_template(entry.template_id).label
    date = parse_iso(entry.selected_at).astimezone(tz).date().isoformat()
    return RagChunk(
        id=f'{entry.brand_id}#hist-{entry.id}',
        source=f'history/{entry.job_id}',
        section=f'{label}・{date}',
        tag='history',
        text=history_text(entry, label, product_name),
    )


def history_text(entry: HistoryEntry, label: str, product_name: str | None) -> str:
    """「商品名稱・模板海報」與審查分數、標題與副標、賣點、正向提示詞的前 120 字；空白的項目省略。

    商品名稱放在第一句的開頭：檢索的查詢字串以「品牌名｜商品名」開頭，同一個商品過去選定的海報較容易被召回。
    """
    copy, render = entry.copy_, entry.render
    poster = f'{product_name}・{label}海報' if product_name else f'{label}海報'
    review = '未經審查評分' if render.score is None else f'審查 {render.score} 分'
    prompt = render.spec.positive_prompt
    if len(prompt) > PROMPT_CHARS:
        prompt = prompt[:PROMPT_CHARS] + '…'
    fields = [
        ('標題', '／'.join(line for line in copy.headline if line)),
        ('副標', copy.subhead),
        ('賣點', '、'.join(copy.features)),
        ('正向提示詞', prompt),
    ]
    return '。'.join([f'{poster}，{review}', *(f'{name}：{value}' for name, value in fields if value)])
```

```python
async def upsert_chunk(session: AsyncSession, brand_id: str, chunk: RagChunk, origin: str, now: datetime) -> None:
    """以 id 新增或取代一個切塊（歷史回寫：重跑不會產生重複的切塊）。已經有時保留第一次寫入的時間，列出的順序不變。"""
    values = {
        'brand_id': brand_id,
        'source': chunk.source,
        'section': chunk.section,
        'tag': chunk.tag,
        'text': chunk.text,
        'origin': origin,
    }
    stmt = insert(BrandChunkRow).values(id=chunk.id, position=0, created_at=now, **values)
    await session.execute(stmt.on_conflict_do_update(index_elements=[BrandChunkRow.id], set_=values))
    await session.commit()
```

改成：

```python


async def write_back(services: Services, history_id: str) -> bool:
    """選定的海報回寫成歷史切塊：以 id upsert 到 PostgreSQL（重跑不會重複），真實版再向量化並寫入 Chroma
    （向量化在品牌鎖之外，寫入在鎖內），最後標記 writtenToRag。歷史紀錄不存在時回傳 False。
    切塊文字帶有商品目前的名稱；商品已刪除時省略。

    出錯時丟出例外，SAQ 記為失敗，api 回傳 writtenToRag: false；已寫入 PostgreSQL 的切塊在下一次同步時補上 Chroma。
    """
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        entry = await history.get_history(s, history_id)
        product = await catalog.get_product(s, entry.product_id) if entry else None
    if entry is None:
        return False
    chunk = history_chunk(entry, product.name if product else None, ZoneInfo(services.settings.timezone))
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        await catalog.upsert_chunk(s, entry.brand_id, chunk, 'history', utc_now())
    if services.rag.mode == 'bge':
        record = await vector_record(services.rag, entry.brand_id, chunk, 'history')
        async with services.locks(entry.brand_id):
            await services.vectors.upsert(entry.brand_id, [record])
    async with services.db.session() as s:
        return await history.mark_written(s, history_id)
```

- [ ] **Step 15: 建立 `web/src/lib/rag/history.ts`**

```ts
import type { HistoryEntry, RagChunk } from '@/types/contract';
import { getTemplate } from '@/lib/compose/templates';

/** 正向提示詞只取前面這麼多字（以 code point 計算），超過時加上「…」 */
export const PROMPT_CHARS = 120;

/** section 的日期以台灣時間計算（與後端 TIMEZONE 的預設值相同） */
const DATE = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: 'Asia/Taipei', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' });

function localDate(iso: string): string {
  const parts = Object.fromEntries(DATE.formatToParts(new Date(iso)).map((p) => [p.type, p.value]));
  return `${parts.year}-${parts.month}-${parts.day}`;
}

/**
 * 「商品名稱・模板海報」與審查分數、標題與副標、賣點、正向提示詞的前 120 字；空白的項目省略。
 * 商品名稱放在第一句的開頭：檢索的查詢字串以「品牌名｜商品名」開頭，同一個商品過去選定的海報較容易被召回。
 */
function historyText(entry: HistoryEntry, label: string, productName: string | null): string {
  const { copy, render } = entry;
  const poster = productName ? `${productName}・${label}海報` : `${label}海報`;
  const review = render.score === null ? '未經審查評分' : `審查 ${render.score} 分`;
  const chars = [...render.spec.positivePrompt];
  const prompt = chars.length > PROMPT_CHARS ? `${chars.slice(0, PROMPT_CHARS).join('')}…` : chars.join('');
  const fields: [string, string][] = [
    ['標題', copy.headline.filter(Boolean).join('／')],
    ['副標', copy.subhead],
    ['賣點', copy.features.join('、')],
    ['正向提示詞', prompt],
  ];
  return [`${poster}，${review}`, ...fields.filter(([, value]) => value).map(([name, value]) => `${name}：${value}`)].join('。');
}

/**
 * 選定的海報 → 歷史回寫切塊（mock 的 selectRender 加進知識庫）：之後的任務檢索得到過去選定的海報。
 * 與後端 backend/app/rag/history.py 的格式相同，修改時兩邊一起改（兩邊的測試比對同一段文字）。
 * productName 為回寫時的商品名稱，商品已刪除時為 null（文字省略商品名稱）。
 */
export function historyChunk(entry: HistoryEntry, productName: string | null): RagChunk {
  const label = getTemplate(entry.templateId).label;
  return {
    id: `${entry.brandId}#hist-${entry.id}`,
    source: `history/${entry.jobId}`,
    section: `${label}・${localDate(entry.selectedAt)}`,
    tag: 'history',
    text: historyText(entry, label, productName),
  };
}
```

### Task 9：系統狀態顯示 Chroma 與 RAG 模型

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/app/api/catalog.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_api_catalog.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes：Task 3 的 `app.rag.state`。api 只匯入這個模組。
- Produces：
  - 系統狀態元件的順序：db、redis、worker、composer、brands、**chroma**、**rag**、gemini、comfyui、cutout。
  - `_chroma(services) -> str | tuple[ComponentState, str]`：
    - 以 httpx 在 `STATUS_TIMEOUT` 內檢查 `{chroma_url}/api/v2/heartbeat`。
    - 正常時顯示網址。

```python
async def test_status_shows_the_rag_model_state(api: LiveApi) -> None:
    cases = [
        ('sim', 'sim', None, 'sim', '模擬檢索：固定的召回與 Rerank 順序'),
        ('bge', 'downloading', None, 'off', '第一次使用，正在下載模型（約 1.2 GB）'),

        ('bge', 'loading', None, 'off', '正在載入模型'),
        ('bge', 'ready', None, 'ok', 'bge-m3＋bge-reranker-v2-m3 已載入'),
        ('bge', 'failed', 'RAG 模型下載失敗：x', 'error', 'RAG 模型下載失敗：x'),
    ]
    for mode, state, error, status, detail in cases:
        message = {'model': mode, 'state': state, 'error': error}
        await api.services.redis.set(RAG_STATE_KEY, json.dumps(message, ensure_ascii=False))
        assert (await status_of(api))['rag'] == {'id': 'rag', 'label': RAG_LABEL, 'status': status, 'detail': detail}
    await api.services.redis.delete(RAG_STATE_KEY)

async def test_status_reads_what_the_rag_engine_reports(api: LiveApi, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    await RagEngine('sim', tmp_path, api.services.redis).prepare_files()
    assert (await status_of(api))['rag']['status'] == 'sim'
    engine = RagEngine('bge', tmp_path, api.services.redis, models=[], load=lambda _: FakeEncoder())
    await engine.prepare_files()
    assert (await status_of(api))['rag']['detail'] == '模型已就緒，第一次檢索時載入'
    await engine.encoder()
    assert (await status_of(api))['rag']['detail'] == 'bge-m3＋bge-reranker-v2-m3 已載入'

    def broken(models_dir: Path) -> FakeEncoder:
        raise RuntimeError('模型檔損毀')

    with pytest.raises(RagError):
        await RagEngine('bge', tmp_path, api.services.redis, models=[], load=broken).encoder()
    component = (await status_of(api))['rag']
    assert (component['status'], component['detail']) == ('error', 'RAG 模型準備失敗：模型檔損毀')


async def test_status_never_fails_on_an_unreadable_rag_state(api: LiveApi) -> None:
    unreadable = [
        'not json',
        '[1, 2]',
        '"ready"',
        json.dumps({'model': 'bge'}),
        json.dumps({'model': 'bge', 'state': 'weird'}),
    ]
    for raw in unreadable:
        await api.services.redis.set(RAG_STATE_KEY, raw)
        res = await api.http.get('/api/status')
        assert res.status_code == 200
        component = {c['id']: c for c in res.json()['components']}['rag']
        assert (component['status'], component['detail']) == ('error', 'RAG 模型狀態無法辨識'), raw
    # 失敗時沒有附上原因
    await api.services.redis.set(RAG_STATE_KEY, json.dumps({'model': 'bge', 'state': 'failed', 'error': None}))
    component = (await status_of(api))['rag']
    assert (component['status'], component['detail']) == ('error', 'RAG 模型準備失敗')
```

```python
async def _chroma(services: ApiServices) -> str | tuple[ComponentState, str]:
    """Chroma 的 heartbeat（只有 worker 寫入 Chroma，api 只檢查它是否回應）；正常時顯示網址。
    RAG_MODE=sim 時不檢查：模擬模式不連 Chroma，沒有啟動 Chroma 的環境（例如 live E2E）不該顯示錯誤。"""
    if services.settings.rag_mode == 'sim':
        return 'sim', '模擬模式不使用向量庫'
    url = services.settings.chroma_url.rstrip('/')
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=STATUS_TIMEOUT) as http:
            res = await http.get(f'{url}/api/v2/heartbeat')
    except httpx.HTTPError as error:
        raise Unavailable(f'連不上 Chroma（{url}）：{failure_reason(error)}') from error
    if res.status_code != 200:
        raise Unavailable(f'Chroma 回應 HTTP {res.status_code}（{url}）')
    return url
```

```python
async def _rag_model(services: ApiServices) -> tuple[ComponentState, str]:
    """worker 寫在 Redis 的 RAG 模型狀態（只讀 app.rag.state，api 不載入 onnxruntime）；
    讀不到、格式不對或不認得的狀態都顯示為 error，不能讓系統狀態整個失敗。"""
    raw = await services.redis.get(RAG_STATE_KEY)
    if raw is None:
        raise Unavailable('worker 尚未回報')
    try:
        state = json.loads(raw)
        name = state['state']
        shown: dict[str, tuple[ComponentState, str]] = {
            'sim': ('sim', '模擬檢索：固定的召回與 Rerank 順序'),
            'downloading': ('off', '第一次使用，正在下載模型（約 1.2 GB）'),
            'standby': ('ok', '模型已就緒，第一次檢索時載入'),
            'loading': ('off', '正在載入模型'),
            'ready': ('ok', 'bge-m3＋bge-reranker-v2-m3 已載入'),
            'failed': ('error', str(state.get('error') or 'RAG 模型準備失敗')),
        }
        return shown[name]
    except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as error:
        raise Invalid('RAG 模型狀態無法辨識') from error
```

### Task 10：Plan 4 延後的項目

本 Task 處理規格第 8 節的 1–5 項：
1. Redis 停止時，WebSocket 改為讀資料庫。
2. 不是 JSON 的 5xx 回應，視為連不上後端。
3. live E2E 啟動前先清理。
4. nginx 設定 `resolver_timeout`。
5. 產生的 migration 要能通過 ruff。

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - 後端：`backend/app/events.py`、`backend/migrations/script.py.mako`、`backend/alembic.ini`
  - 前端：`web/src/lib/api/http/client.ts`、`web/e2e-live/global-setup.ts`、`web/nginx.conf`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_events.py`、`backend/tests/test_api_jobs.py`、`web/src/lib/api/http/client.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces：
  - `app.events`：
    - `POLL_INTERVAL = 1.0`；`REDIS_DOWN = (RedisError, OSError)`。
    - `EventStore._subscribe(job_id) -> PubSub | None`；`_close(pubsub)` 只呼叫 `aclose()`，不送 UNSUBSCRIBE。
    - 訂閱失敗，或之後讀取失敗時，記錄警告並改為每秒讀資料庫，直到任務結束；結束時仍以 1000 關閉連線。
  - 測試輔助：`tests.test_events.DownPubSub(subscribe_fails=..., read_fails_after=0)`、`publish_down`。
  - `HttpApiClient`：
    - `backendError(res) -> ApiError | null` 取代 `toApiError`。
    - 不是後端 JSON 的 5xx（例如 nginx 的 502／504、`next dev` 的 500）轉成 `network`，連線狀態顯示斷線；後端自己的 JSON 500 不受影響。
  - `alembic.ini` 的 `post_write_hooks`：產生 migration 後執行 `ruff check --fix` 與 `ruff format`。`alembic.ini` 維持 ASCII。

```python
class DownPubSub:
    """Redis 停止時的訂閱：subscribe 失敗，或訂閱成功後讀取時失敗（read_fails_after 次之後）。"""

    def __init__(self, *, subscribe_fails: bool, read_fails_after: int = 0) -> None:
        self.subscribe_fails = subscribe_fails
        self.reads_left = read_fails_after
        self.closed = False

    async def subscribe(self, *channels: str) -> None:
        if self.subscribe_fails:
            raise RedisConnectionError('Error 10061 connecting to 127.0.0.1:6379.')

    async def get_message(self, *, ignore_subscribe_messages: bool, timeout: float) -> None:
        if self.reads_left == 0:
            raise RedisConnectionError('Connection closed by server.')
        self.reads_left -= 1
        await asyncio.sleep(timeout)

    async def aclose(self) -> None:
        self.closed = True


async def publish_down(job_id: str, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    raise RedisConnectionError('Connection refused')


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    'down',
    [{'subscribe_fails': True}, {'subscribe_fails': False, 'read_fails_after': 1}],
    ids=['subscribe-fails', 'read-fails-later'],
)
async def test_stream_polls_the_database_every_second_when_redis_is_down(
    store: EventStore, down: dict[str, Any], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture
) -> None:
    pubsub = DownPubSub(**down)
    monkeypatch.setattr(store.redis, 'pubsub', lambda: pubsub)
    monkeypatch.setattr(store, 'publish', publish_down)
    await store.append('job_e', JobAcceptedEvent(type='job.accepted', job_id='job_e'))
    received: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    async def consume() -> None:
        async for message in store.stream('job_e'):
            received.append(message)

    with caplog.at_level('WARNING', logger='app.events'):
        task = asyncio.create_task(consume())
        await asyncio.sleep(0.2)
        # 已經在資料庫的事件立刻補送；之後的事件不經過 Redis，最多一個間隔後從資料庫讀到
        assert [m['seq'] for m in received] == [1]
        await store.append('job_e', status('queued'))
        await store.append('job_e', log('working'))
        async with asyncio.timeout(3 * POLL_INTERVAL):
            while len(received) < 3:
                await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        assert not task.done()
        await store.append('job_e', status('done', 1))
        await asyncio.wait_for(task, 3 * POLL_INTERVAL)
    assert [(m['seq'], m['type']) for m in received] == [
        (1, 'job.accepted'),
        (2, 'job.status'),
        (3, 'log'),
        (4, 'job.status'),
    ]
    assert pubsub.closed
    assert '改為每秒從資料庫讀取' in caplog.text
```
